- **v5 overlay pass REVERTED (superseded by the above):** Joe had **started painting the new Shin-Tokyo with the v4 pair**, and the v5 rebuild corrupted the painted map — green-tinted tiles + "erased" cells. **ROOT CAUSE, now a HARD RULE: once ANY map is painted with a tileset, rebuilds that insert/remove/resize pieces MID-LIST reshuffle every metatile id and re-pack the palettes — painted cells then reference wrong art and wrong palette slots.** Both scripts restored to exact v4 (deterministic pipeline → identical 286t/304m + 448t/368m binaries; the painted map renders correctly again). **Protocol for future MiddleDistrict additions:** (1) append new pieces ONLY at the END of the build list, (2) after building, DIFF the 13 palette files against the previous build — if any palette changed, painted cells will tint and Joe must approve a repaint first. The v5 content (storefront base, metro stair patch + bin, transparent hole, transparent car) is drafted in this section's history and can be re-added append-only on request.

Each overlay ships in road + sidewalk ground variants (`wires_road/side`, `wire_drop_road/side`, `pillar_road/side`). True per-map-layer transparency doesn't exist in the 2-layer engine — ground variants are the standard workaround; more base variants are one-line adds. No garbage bag exists in the render. ### MiddleDistrict tileset — reverse-sliced from a map render (2026-07-30)
- **`gTileset_MiddleDistrict`** (456t/464m, secondary, pairs with General): Joe wanted the buildings from Emeiry's **"Middle District"** map render for the Odisea project (deviantart.com/emeiry/art/Middle-District-897051289). **No tile sheet was ever released** (confirmed on the DA page), so `build_middle_district.py` reverse-slices the render itself — it's 1952×1792, perfectly 16-grid-aligned at 1x, downloaded to `tilesets_raw/middle_district_by_emeiry.png`.
- **CREDIT OBLIGATION: the render is CC BY-NC-SA 3.0 — credit "Emeiry (Middle District / Odisea by ekat99)" in the hack's credits.** Non-commercial fan hack use is within license.
- **The technique:** a rendered map at 1x IS a metatile grid — slice 16×16 cells from chosen rects. The catch: **baked lighting gradients make nearly every tile unique** (13664 cells → 6418 unique; the arcade facade alone = 401 tiles). Fix: **pre-quantize the render to 96 colors (median-cut)** before slicing — gradient micro-variants collapse (facade 163→76 tiles) and palettes relax. `middle_district_q96.png` is the actual import source.
- **In:** glowing red-window facade, green-brick tower face, roof kit, lattice wall, METRO entrance (neon sign), fan wall, crosswalk, bollard lamp, grass planter. **Out (didn't fit 512):** the pokeball arcade (401t), fountain (102t), street trees, hedges — these want the **custom-primary companion** (primary = streetscape ground kit, secondary = buildings) if Joe wants the full Odisea streetscape on one map.
- Note: `odisea_outdoors_by_ekat99` (already in tilesets_raw) is a DIFFERENT slice of the same project (storefronts/industrial pipes) — importable later the normal way.

### Three new tilesets imported (2026-07-30, scale-corrected same day)
- **`gTileset_CityBlocks` / `gTileset_DragonKeepers` / `gTileset_SumpRats`** — three new secondaries (pair with gTileset_General), built by `tilesets_raw/build_scripts/build_{city_blocks,dragon_keepers,sump_rats}.py`, registered in the usual three files, ROM builds green. Porymap sees them immediately.
- **SCALE (Joe's double-size report, root-caused):** DragonKeepers art is **2x** (verified 100% uniform 2×2 blocks) and SumpRats **2x** (96.8%) — both now import with `half=True`; **doors = 1 tile**. The city collage is **TRUE 1x** (its PC door measures exactly 16px; per-building uniformity 45–68% = flat shading, not doubling) — it was **never rescaled**; its buildings are just drawn large. **Verify scale with the uniform-2×2-block test + a door crop BEFORE importing any new sheet.**
- **Contents as built (Joe's curated pass, 2026-07-30):** **CityBlocks** (495t/464m, ON HOLD — Joe may not use it; untouched) = modular tower kit + blue octagon tower + orange container tower + red PC shop. **DragonKeepers** (485t/216m) = tower house + courtyard house + dragon statue + arch + **all 3 egg-incubating plates + pillar + nest pillar** (fenced house and banner gave way — the plates/pillars cost ~180 tiles). **SumpRats** (395t/248m) = green + red containers, A-frame, **trapezoid shack**, yurt, drain pipes + vent grate, mushroom barrels + STR soda can, one flower pot, SUMP graffiti, skull flag, **trubbish-like critters ×2 + grimer as static decor** (terrain/pokeballs/signs/crates all cut by Joe's call).
- **CRUNCH FIX:** the sump art is 2x-with-stray-pixels (96.8%); plain NEAREST halving kept top-left pixels → noise, and maxed palettes crushed the browns green. `tilesetgen.py` halving is now a **majority-vote 2×2 downsample** (identical output for perfectly-uniform sheets), and the content cuts freed palette room — quality confirmed via renders.
- **True-1x budget numbers:** one dragon house = 131–144 tiles; sump full sheet = 1040 tiles ≈ 2 secondaries. **NOT imported:** dragon house_lean, fire plates, pillars, nest perch, small statue; sump bush band, olive/blue containers, patchwork+scrap huts, gear table, shelves, cans, misc props; city mega-complexes/mall/civic/dept store. A **custom PRIMARY** (precedent build_r224_primary.py) doubles a map's budget if a full town map happens.
- **SumpRats sheet map (for future imports):** src y2184–2228 = "SUMP RATS" graffiti logos; **y2250–2390 = CRITTER SPRITES (grimer/crab mons — NOT tileset material**, first import accidentally included them from mislabeled component boxes); y2400–2462 barrels trio; y2484–2782 market block (pots rows, cans, crates, shelves, skull banner). Sprites in the zip (`Characters/`, `Trainers/`, `Items/` at `tilesets_raw/SumpRats_extracted/`) await OW/trainer wiring.
- **Pipeline gotchas learned this run:** objects wider than `sel_width` are silently right-clipped (probe at real width!); wide sel_width burns metatiles on row padding — shelf-pack side by side; per-metatile behaviors (pond water etc.) are editable in porymap's Tileset Editor, no rebuild; scripts self-locate paths (WSL-runnable); **never trust semantic labels from a component pass — render the region and LOOK before importing.**

### Playtest round 3 (2026-07-29 evening, untested)
- **Partner-battle snags now fill the party first:** root cause of "doubles snags go to PC despite an open slot" — partner multis restore `gPlayerParty` from a backup after battle, so the old code sent every snag to the PC. Now `BS_WotGiveSnaggedMon` writes into the **backup party itself** (`GetSavedPlayerPartyMon`/`SavePlayerPartyMon` — the vanilla partner-catch machinery at battle_script_commands.c:4510 blessed this pattern): first open slot joins the party after the restore, PC only when full. Message chooser follows (party vs PC line).
- **Shadow patrol pools (research-dependent) + re-entry rerolls:** `WotTryRandomizeShadowPatrol` (battle_main.c, hooked at the top of `CreateNPCTrainerParty`) rebuilds GORO/UME/ROKU's teams at battle start — 3 distinct random picks, Lv43/43/44, all Shadow. Pool = **research TAKEN** (`FLAG_ACT3_RESEARCH_STOLEN`): Rillaboom/Haxorus/Melmetal/Ursaluna/Rhyperior/Annihilape; **research DESTROYED**: Weavile/Honchkrow/Toxicroak/Drapion/Corvisquire/Centiskorch/Tinkaton/Arctozolt/Archeops. City ON_TRANSITION (red-alert branch) clears their defeat flags, so **leaving + re-entering re-arms all three patrols with fresh rolls**. The debug "Shadow: single" battle (TRAINER_NATE) now also rolls from the pool.
- **Surf teach-free FOR REAL:** the earlier scrcmd waiver never ran because `GetInteractedWaterScript` (field_control_avatar.c) gates on **`PartyHasMonWithSurf()` in C before any script fires** — dropped it; badge + surfable water + follower check remain. Waterfall/Dive C gates were already badge-only. **Lesson: overworld interactions can be double-gated in C — grep `field_control_avatar.c` before assuming a script-side fix took.**
- **Tower guardian duo:** the four grunt trainers are REPLACED — Street = **WHITETIGER** (Expert, Lv44-46: shiny Incineroar / Chien-Pao / Absol @ Absolite / Landorus-Therian / Meowscarada / **Shadow Raikou** ace) on `TRAINER_BLAKE`; Mid = **SUZAKU** (Expert, Lv49-51: Talonflame / Altaria @ Altarianite / Fezandipiti / Blaziken / Moltres-Galar / **Shadow Ho-Oh** ace) on `TRAINER_SYLVIA`. Floor gates now check the single guardian; each floor map has one COOLTRAINER object (sight 4). **`TRAINER_KAYLA` (462) and `TRAINER_NICHOLAS` (~650s) are FREED** — their party blocks remain but nothing references them; add to the safe-id pool. Four-beasts theming (Byakko/Suzaku) leaves room for Genbu/Seiryu guardians later if wanted.

### Map/surf/badge fixes (2026-07-29 latest, untested)
- **"Shin-Tokyo reverts to the old version" ROOT CAUSE — FIXED:** the red-alert state used a **second, copied layout** (`LAYOUT_SHIN_TOKYO_RED_ALERT`), and that copy went stale the moment the city was repainted in Porymap — **1157 tiles** differed. After the jailbreak the game swapped to the old copy. **The duplicate layout is DELETED** (blockdata, layouts.json entry, and the `setmaplayoutindex` call). **ONE layout — `LAYOUT_SHIN_TOKYO`, the one Porymap edits — is now the source of truth**, so map edits always show. The tide beat is gated in SCRIPT instead: the south surf trigger refuses with a "nothing crosses while the tower draws power" message until `FLAG_ACT3_RED_ALERT`. **NEVER re-introduce a copied layout for a state change** — if the wanted-poster art needs a visual swap later, do it with runtime metatile edits on the single layout.
- **Surf ROOT CAUSE — FIXED:** the repainted city (now **General + FearTown**, was NightMarket + AshlandTrees) used metatile **0x147** for its south water — a General tile whose behavior is **MB_NORMAL**, so the engine never saw water and Surf could not start. FearTown has no water metatiles at all. Converted the 229 elev-1 `0x147` tiles to **`0x170`** — General's canonical ocean water (`MB_OCEAN_WATER`), the same tile Celebi Island already uses. Celebi's water was already correct, so its failure was the shared unlock: **Surf needs `FLAG_BADGE05_GET` (Distortion Badge)** and previously failed SILENTLY. `EventScript_UseSurf` now says why ("no badge you carry gives you the right to cross it yet"). **If a repaint breaks surf again, check the metatile's BEHAVIOR, not just collision/elevation.**
- **Snag nickname prompt REMOVED** (black-screened: the vanilla caught-mon naming flow expects the wild-CAUGHT battle-end path). Snagged mons are still renameable any time from the party menu. `wotcollectsnagged`/`wotgivesnagged` are unchanged otherwise.
- **Badges on the trainer card:** the card has a **hardcoded WoT badge list** (`src/trainer_card.c`) that was missing the two newest. Now: Ember(01) · Flint(03) · Lantern(04) · Distortion(05) · **Underground(06)** · **Tower(08)**. The continue/save-menu count reads `FLAG_BADGE01..08` directly, so it already included both. **Add every future badge to that list.**

### Jailbreak revision 2 + HM system (2026-07-29 later, untested)
- **ARREST LOOP ROOT CAUSE (fixed):** script contexts do NOT survive warps — the old scene warped mid-script and died, leaving `FLAG_ACT3_ARREST_DONE` unset, so the trigger ring re-fired forever. The street scene now ENDS at the warp with the flag already set, and the **oni-disguise reveal + Zoroark + defector briefing + Stop the Machine quest** replay as a **RebelHideout ON_FRAME scene** (`FLAG_WOT_HIDEOUT_REVEAL_DONE` 0x2B0; scene ONI/operative/ZOROARK = hideout objects 5–7 on the arrest-NPCs hide flag; the static operative (obj 1) hides until the reveal introduces her — `FLAG_HIDE_REBEL_OPERATIVE` 0x2B1, managed every transition). **LESSON (repeated twice now): never write script logic after a warp.**
- **Mikmanc rewrite:** the Snag Machine was **stolen from Mutrid's own lab** on his way into the cell (no more "everything the rebellion has left") — handover first, then the how-it-works explainer ("any BALL can rip a SHADOW off their team; it only answers to Shadows"). At the first squad trigger he now calls it: "Here's our chance — use the SNAG MACHINE!" and **side quest "Harness Darkness"** starts (id 10, Snag-Machine item icon, toast + quest log; completion NOT yet wired — suggest first purification).
- **Shiv inmate:** cell beside Mikmanc's (11,3, BLACK_BELT): "I made a shiv to help us escape" → **Quick Claw** → bolts down the block and is gone for good (`FLAG_WOT_INMATE_GONE` 0x2B2).
- **HM SYSTEM — HMs are trainer abilities now:** once the badge unlocks a classic field move (Cut/Flash/Rock Smash/Strength/Surf/Fly/Dive/Waterfall), it works with **no party mon needing the move** — single-point waiver in `ScrCmd_checkfieldmove` (HM range only; Dig/Teleport/etc. still need the real move). The "{mon} used X!" pose banner is **skipped globally** (`FldEff_FieldMoveShowMonInit` short-circuits) and the mon-name announce lines were stripped from the cut/rock-smash/strength/surf/waterfall/dive scripts — you confirm the prompt and it just happens. Flag next free: **0x2B3+**.
- **START MENU → "HMS" is a real submenu** (appears after Badge 1; `wot_hms.inc` + `dynmultichoice`): a cursor list of only the moves you've unlocked, plus CANCEL. **FLY is USED from here** — pick it and the region map opens (`WotOpenFlyMap` special → `CB2_OpenFlyMap`, the same entry the debug Fly utility uses). Every other HM is used by walking into the world, so selecting one shows a one-line reminder of where it applies. Unlock checks go through `WotIsHMUnlocked` so the badge mapping stays in `field_move.c` only.
- **BLACK-SCREEN ROOT CAUSE (start-menu scripts) — REAL FIX:** `HandleStartMenuInput` calls **`FadeScreen(FADE_TO_BLACK, 0)` on selection for every callback except a hardcoded whitelist** (Save / Exit / Debug / Safari retire / Pyramid retire) — because those are the only vanilla entries that stay on the field; everything else replaces the screen. An in-field script menu that isn't whitelisted gets the map faded out from under it: black screen, menu drawn briefly over it, black again. **Fix = add the callback to that exclusion list** (`src/start_menu.c`, the `gMenuCallback != ...` chain). The callback body itself is just the Safari shape: `RemoveExtraStartMenuWindows(); HideStartMenu(); ScriptContext_SetupScript(...)` with the script doing its own lockall/releaseall. **Any future start-menu entry that runs a script in place needs the same exemption.**

### Ball target selector + jail red alert + debug harness (2026-07-29, untested)
- **DOUBLES SNAG ROOT CAUSE (fixed):** the real blocker was upstream of the battle — `GetBallThrowableState()` in `item_use.c` returns `BALL_THROW_UNABLE_TWO_MONS` ("Cannot throw a ball! There are two Pokémon out there!") whenever both foes are alive, **in the bag**, so the throw never reached the battle-side targeting fix. Vanilla blocks it purely because it can't tell which foe you meant.
- **Manual ball-target selector (new feature):** the block is removed; instead, choosing a ball with two foes alive now opens a **target cursor** (`WotHandleInputChooseBallTarget`, `battle_controller_player.c`) reusing the move-target UI — the chosen foe's sprite highlights and its healthbox bounces. **D-pad** switches foes, **A** throws, **B** cancels (ball returns to the bag, action menu reopens). The pick lands in `gBattleStruct->wotBallTarget` (stored +1 so a zeroed struct means "auto") and `Cmd_handleballthrow` honours it, falling back to the Shadow-preferring auto-aim. Quick-throw (`B_LAST_USED_BALL`) stays single-target-only — it has no prompt — so use the bag in doubles.
- **Jail RED ALERT (after the cell scene):** freeing Mikmanc now wakes the block — SE_BANG, the darkness **animates away and the lights stay on** (ON_TRANSITION's dark branch is skipped once `FLAG_ACT3_MIKMANC_FREED` is set), a shutter slams, then the **klaxon: 4 red screen pulses + alarm beep** (new blocking special `WotRedAlertFlash`, palette-fade based; pair with `waitstate`), and Mikmanc calls the run. **The front door is sealed** from that moment — a bounce trigger on the approach tile (12,18) keeps the player off the warp tile entirely, so the only way on is the west service run. **MUSIC TODO (Joe):** wants a Metal-Gear-style track here — import the track, add a `MUS_*` id, then drop `playbgm` into `ShinTokyoJail_EventScript_CellAfter` at the alert beat.
- **Debug test harness — debug menu → Scripts:** **"Shadow: single"** (slot 7) starts a single battle vs a Shadow patroller; **"Shadow: doubles"** (slot 8) starts the jail's 2v1 partner double vs JUZO+Mikmanc. Both hand out 20 Ultra Balls + the Snag Machine and clear the trainer flag, so they're infinitely repeatable. Between them they exercise: tint, healthbox flame, aura announce, snag waiver, target selector, right-slot snagging, PC delivery in partner battles, nickname prompt, heart gauge, Shadow Boost, EXP lock.

### Playtest round 2 fixes + Shadow mechanics (2026-07-29, untested)
- **Bad Egg ROOT CAUSE (engine bug, fixed + regression-tested):** `MON_DATA_OT_ID` sits before `MON_DATA_ENCRYPT_SEPARATOR`, so `SetBoxMonData` wrote it WITHOUT re-encrypting — but otId is half the substruct encryption key. Snagged trainer mons are the only mons whose OT ever changes → instant checksum garbage → Bad Egg. Fix: OT_ID writes now route through the decrypt/re-encrypt envelope (re-keys the data); mon creation writes personality/otId directly pre-seal. **`test/wot_shadow.c`** covers it — run `make check TESTS='(WoT)'`.
- **Doubles snag targeting (fixed):** the ball always aimed at the LEFT enemy slot, so a right-slot Shadow was unsnaggable and even a Master Ball got trainer-blocked. `Cmd_handleballthrow` now aims at a living Shadow (left preferred, then right). Both-Shadows-out = left first (no manual ball-targeting UI yet).
- **Deoxys now despawns live on capture** (`removeobject`); still persists on defeat/flee.
- **Tower rewire for Joe's resized maps:** all stair/exit/door warp destinations updated to land beside his repositioned triggers (Street 9×12 / Mid 13×14 / Command 15×14 / Top 16×14; district door → Street (4,8)); city⇄Celebi(50×45)⇄Prototype surf destinations updated ((10,43), (1,36)). **Floor gating:** each floor's stairs bounce until all its trainers are beaten (Command's lift already keys on the badge).
- **Shadow mechanics batch:**
  - **Shadow Boost** — canon power tier: any Shadow attacker's damage ×1.2 (`ApplyModifiersAfterDmgRoll`, both sides, AI-aware).
  - **EXP lock** — Shadow mons gain no EXP until purified (XD rule; clean skip in `Cmd_getexp`).
  - **Switch-in aura announce** — "{mon} is wreathed in a Shadow aura!" once per send-in, either side (`ABILITYEFFECT_ON_SWITCHIN` + `wotAuraAnnounced` latch cleared in `SwitchInClearSetData`).
  - **Nickname prompt on delivery** — the snag loop now runs the vanilla caught-mon naming flow per mon BEFORE the give (`BS_WotCollectSnaggedMons` points `gBattlerTarget` at the slot; new `BS_WotGiveSnaggedMon`/`wotgivesnagged` completes the hand-over).
  - **Purified battle indicator** — gold light icon in the healthbox slot for National-Ribbon mons (ribbon only comes from the shrine), same system as the Shadow flame.
- **DEFERRED with plans:** TYPE_SHADOW + Shadow moves (real system: type-chart row, move data, moveset swap + restore on purify — own session; Shadow Boost carries the canon power tier meanwhile) · party-menu shadow/pure markers (cleanest route: add SHADOW/PURE frames to the status-icon sheet and show them via `SetPartyMonAilmentGfx` when no ailment; purity is meanwhile visible in battle icons + the ribbon on the summary screen).
- **Shadow battle-sprite ART:** neither this repo nor the `~/pkm-shadow` fork ships shadow-variant battle sprites (the fork only had its heart-gauge UI). Options: import Joe's artwork per species, or an engine-side purple tint/aura overlay pass (no per-species art needed).
- Also: `startquest`/`completequest` legacy duplicate macro removed (was breaking the TEST build via raw-gas inclusion).

### Shadow system — Phase 3, presentation (DONE 2026-07-28, untested)
- **Snag strings:** success now prints `STRINGID_WOTGOTCHASNAGGED` "Gotcha! {mon} was snagged away!" — the vanilla caught-string's `{PLAY_BGM MUS_CAUGHT}` would have permanently replaced the battle theme mid-fight (bug, fixed).
- **Silent snag exit:** `BattleScript_FaintBattler` branches via new `wotjumpifsnaggedfaint` (native `BS_WotJumpIfSnaggedFaint`): snagged enemy mons skip faint cry + "fainted!" message, keep the faint animation/cleanup. Normal faints untouched.
- **Battle-end delivery messages:** `BS_WotCollectSnaggedMons` reworked into a script loop (`BattleScript_WotDeliverSnagged`, macro `wotcollectsnagged`) — per mon: heal, dex flags, give, then prints "The snagged {mon} was added to your party!" / "…was transferred to the PC!" (`gWotSnagDeliveryStringIds`). Both victory paths now `call` it.
- **Capture-anim snag branch** (`SpriteCB_Ball_Capture_Step`): in trainer battles the success anim no longer runs `m4aMPlayAllStop()`+caught jingle (battle BGM keeps playing), and no longer destroys the target's battler sprite at frame 315 — that used a by-then-stale sprite id that could have wiped the opponent's NEXT sent-out mon (bug, fixed). Master Ball guaranteed path included (→ canon Jirachi finale).

### 2026-08-03 (later still) — BOSS INTRO CARD system, first target: General Edwards (BUILT green, untested)
- **New reusable system** per `boss_intro_card_handoff.md`: `src/boss_intro.c` + `include/boss_intro.h` + `include/constants/boss_intro.h` (BOSS_INTRO_EDWARDS=0). Script side: **`bossintro BOSS_INTRO_X`** macro (event.inc) — callnative + **embedded waitstate** (special+waitstate shape; the task's final state calls `ScriptContext_Enable()` exactly once — never add another waitstate after the macro).
- **The beat:** SE cue + optional `FadeOutBGM(4)` at frame 0 → tint fade (`BeginNormalPaletteFade`, mask = PALETTES_ALL minus the card's two OBJ palettes, deep purple RGB(6,2,12) y=10) → portrait eases in from the right (frame 6+, reuses npc_portrait art via new `GetNpcPortraitGfx()` accessor, own pal tag 0x4B22) → 256×32 banner eases in from the left (frame 14+, OBJ subsprite assembly — 4×64×32 pieces, tag 0x4B20; **NOT BG-offset based**, BG0 stays untouched) → on settle: **SE_MUGSHOT slam** + 192×32 pre-rendered name appears (3×64×32 subsprites, tag 0x4B21, shares banner pal) + 2-frame white flash → hold 90 frames (**A/B skips** after frame 30) → exit slides + tint lift → frees ALL tags/sprites → script resumes. Ease = velocity ×3/4 decay (min 2); exit accelerates ×5/4 (cap 40).
- **Data-driven:** `sBossIntroData[]` row = portraitId, name sheet, 2 SEs, holdFrames, tint, cutMusic. A future boss = new constant + table row + one 192×32 name PNG.
- **Art:** `graphics/boss_intro/banner.png` (256×32 slanted black slab, gold rules, red chevron, hatch texture) + `name_edwards.png` ("GENERAL EDWARDS", Impact, sheared, white/gold/black — shares banner.pal; both `.4bpp.smol` via INCGFX, loaded with `LoadCompressedSpriteSheet`). Generated programmatically — Joe can drop replacement PNGs with the same sizes/palette anytime.
- **Wired at the SennenAct2 platform gate (Edwards):** defeated-gate → plain chat (card never re-fires); else lockall → one-liner (namebox + auto portrait; the existing "GENERAL EDWARDS. Nobody boards." line) → closemessage → `bossintro` → `trainerbattle_no_intro` (the line was the intro) → post-battle line → releaseall. Works from both sight-approach and interact.
- **Debug → Scripts → 6** = card smoke test (replaced a stale Act-1 quest poke); run 5× for the VRAM/palette-leak check.
- **Gotchas encoded:** guards on `gPaletteFade.active` before starting AND before finishing the lift; double-trigger guard skips the card instead of deadlocking; `RGB()` lives in `constants/rgb.h` and `BlendPalette` in `util.h` (NOT palette.h).
- **PLAYTEST FIX (Joe's screenshot, 2026-08-03):** the banner/name rendered SCRAMBLED — multi-piece OBJ sheets MUST be converted with metatile args matching the subsprite piece size (`-mwidth 8 -mheight 4` for 64×32 pieces), same rule as the OW walking sheets' `-mwidth 2 -mheight 4`. Without them gbagfx serializes row-major across the whole image and every piece draws interleaved garbage. ALSO: changing INCGFX args does NOT dirty the generated `.4bpp/.smol` — delete the intermediates (or touch the png) or make serves the stale conversion.
- **PLAYTEST FIX 2:** the nameplate drew BEHIND the banner (both at subpriority 0 → creation order decided). OBJ-vs-OBJ layering = subpriority, LOWER draws in front (sort key → earlier OAM slot). Card layering now explicit: name 0 (front) / portrait 1 / banner 2.
- **EDWARDS BATTLE UPGRADE (2026-08-03):** custom **battle front pic** (`edwards battle sprite.jpg` → `graphics/trainers/front_pics/general_edwards.png`, TRAINER_PIC_FRONT_GENERAL_EDWARDS + TRAINER_SPRITE row; `Pic: General Edwards` in the party file). **Team = Shadow Charizard Lv41 (lead) + Shadow Empoleon Lv36** (both have custom shadow art; the "sheathed in shadow aura" switch-in line fires automatically for any IS_SHADOW battler — engine-wide, no gating). **Battle theme = MUS_DP_VS_UXIE_MESPRIT_AZELF, starting AT THE SLAM:** new `battleBGM` field in BossIntroData → `m4aMPlayAllStop + m4aSongNumStart` when the nameplate hits, sets `gWotBossIntroPrimed`. battle_setup consumes it: `GetTrainerBattleTransition` returns **B_TRANSITION_WHITE_BARS_FADE** (quick white-out instead of the long class transition = "skips the battle intro animation") and `CreateBattleStartTask` SKIPS `PlayMapChosenOrBattleBGM` + clears the flag — the theme runs unbroken from card into battle. Track choice: m4aSongNumStart directly (NOT PlayNewMapMusic) so the map-music bookkeeping is untouched and the field BGM restores normally after the fight.
- **PLAYTEST FIX 3 (music doubling):** the old track relaunched OVER the boss theme — the trainer-approach **encounter jingle queues itself in the map-music state machine** (sound.c state 6: "once the BGM stops, play the queued song"), and the slam's `m4aMPlayAllStop` SATISFIED that wait → MapMusicMain replayed the old song on top. Fix: `cutMusic` now uses **FadeOutMapMusic(4)** (fades AND clears current/next/state) and the slam calls **ResetMapMusic()** before AllStop + SongNumStart, plus `gMPlayInfo_BGM.fadeOI = 0` after starting (an in-flight m4a fade counter survives a song start and would silently fade the NEW theme to zero). RULE: whenever scripted music must own the speakers, neutralize the map-music state machine — a raw stop is an invitation for it to relaunch whatever it had queued.
- **Team rev 2:** lead = **Shadow Onix Lv35** (replacing Charizard), Shadow Empoleon Lv36 second — both with custom shadow art.
- **Tuning rev (2026-08-03 late):** card hold **90→180 frames** (~3s; A/B skip unchanged); battle background = **BATTLE_ENVIRONMENT_VOLCANO** (same molten-volcano bg as the summit boss) via the `GetBattleEnvironmentOverride()` per-trainer pattern in battle_bg.c.
- **Team FINAL (Joe's call — Act 2 is the MEGA act, shadows stay in Act 3):** **Tyranitar @ Tyranitarite Lv41 lead (mega-evolves turn 1)**, Corviknight 36, Blastoise 38, Lucario 37, Volcarona 39, Falinks 35 — full six, no shadows, so no aura line here (correct theming).
- **PLAYTEST FIX 4 (music doubling, round 2 — the REAL trigger):** `EventScript_DoNoIntroTrainerBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:52) runs `special PlayTrainerEncounterMusic` the moment `trainerbattle` executes — i.e. exactly at card end — which PlayNewMapMusic'd the encounter jingle over the boss theme (the BGM player being silent let the state machine fire it instantly). `PlayTrainerEncounterMusic` now returns immediately while `gWotBossIntroPrimed` is set. **Every trainerbattle mode routes through that special** — any future scripted-music beat that flows into a trainer battle needs this same gate (it's in place now, keyed on the primed flag).
- **Open per spec §10 (Joe):** subtitle line under the name (system currently name-only), banner color language per faction, hardware-brightness check (EZ-Flash reads darker — tint may need lightening).

### 2026-08-07 (later still) — STORM AUDIO WAS THE REAL CULPRIT + shake fix (BUILT green 14:43, untested)
- **Joe confirmed the rainstorm is behind the SE chaos. Root cause:** SennenAct2 is WEATHER_DOWNPOUR — its rain loop plays on the DEDICATED ambient SE player (`IsSpecialSEPlaying`), runs continuously UNDER everything, and hogs DirectSound hardware channels → scene SEs and the card slam get channel-starved/mangled. Normal `playse` never replaces it (different player).
- **New system: `WotSuppressWeatherSE` special** (field_weather.c + registered END of specials.inc): stops the current rain loop cleanly (`PlayRainStoppingSoundEffect`) and sets a mute that gates `SetRainStrengthFromSoundEffect`'s PlaySE + the thunder claps in `UpdateThunderSound` (field_weather_effect.c). **Weather VISUALS continue.** The mute is cleared in `StartWeather()` — every map load/battle return restarts the storm audio automatically, so no script cleanup is needed and it can never leak past the scene. Called at the START of both the assault cutscene and the Edwards ambush.
- **"No screen shake" fix:** two problems: vanilla `ShakeCamera` plays its OWN `PlaySE(SE_M_STRENGTH)` — which replaced the explosion boom on the normal SE player (audio), and ±3px panning reads as nothing under heavy rain (visual). New **`WotShakeCameraSilent`** special = same task, no SE; ambush now shakes at **vertical 6 / horizontal 2 / 10 shakes / delay 3** (violent, unmissable). Vanilla ShakeCamera untouched (other scenes rely on its thud).
- RULE: on weather maps, ANY audio-sensitive cutscene should `special WotSuppressWeatherSE` first.
### 2026-08-13 (round 6) — CI cleanup: repo checks ALL GREEN (verified via Actions API)
- Every red X predated this session's releases; build-emerald was green throughout. Three causes, three fixes (master a8956477cc):
  1. **docs_validate = Permission denied**: the FIRST WoT commit (Windows, core.filemode off) stripped +x from 1,093 files; `.github/docs_validate/inclusive_summary.py` is the one CI executes directly. **GOTCHA: restoring it took two commits — `git update-index --chmod=+x` then `git add -A` RE-STAGES the file and silently reverts the mode.** Stage the chmod alone and commit immediately.
  2. **test job dropped** from build.yml (like the FRLG jobs): the upstream engine test battery has failed since WoT began and asserts behavior the hack changes on purpose. Gate = build-emerald + release.
  3. **Docs workflow → workflow_dispatch only**: its mdBook Pages deploy is rejected while Pages serves flatfootlabs.com from main:/docs — and in Actions mode it would REPLACE the site with engine docs. Never let it run implicitly.
- Post-push CI run on a8956477cc: **conclusion SUCCESS** (checked via the public Actions API).

### 2026-09-14 (round 79) — SNOW ENCOUNTERS + NIGHT TINT FIXED; FROSTWOOD GYM LOCKED BEHIND REDFATALITY'S EPIC PASS; 3x BALL THROWS
- **Snow ground started wild battles, and the ice never slid — both my round-67 bug.** `fix_snow_behaviours.py` parsed `metatile_behaviors.h` with a regex that skipped any enum line with a trailing comment. `MB_INTERIOR_DEEP_WATER, // ...` has one, so every later value came out **one low**: ice was written as 31 = `MB_POKEMART_SIGN` (the mart-sign text Joe saw), snow ground as 36 = `MB_ASHGRASS` (an encounter tile). `declaw_munen_water.py` hardcoded the same wrong 36 for clones 400-406. **True values, from a compiled probe:** MB_ICE 32, MB_ASHGRASS 36, MB_FOOTPRINTS 37. `correct_snow_behaviours_off_by_one.py` changed only the 22 SnowStone entries still holding a wrong value (31→32, 36→37). It left the 18 Joe had already re-set in porymap: 8 ice at 32, 8 snow at MB_NORMAL, 2 clones at MB_PUDDLE. Both old scripts are fixed so a re-run can't write the bad values. **`MB_FOOTPRINTS` no longer has `TILE_FLAG_HAS_ENCOUNTERS`** (`src/metatile_behavior.c`). Vanilla paints no footprints tiles, and WoT uses it for snow, including Joe's General 0x05E / 0x108. Melting Mile encounters now come only from its tall grass (0x35F).
- **Night lighting glitch:** crossing a map CONNECTION re-tints only the secondary palettes [6,12]. Walnut Woods was `MAP_TYPE_NONE` (no natural light) next to Melting Mile's `MAP_TYPE_ROUTE`, so General palettes [0,5] kept the previous map's day/night state. That left the snow ground (General 0x05E) bright under tinted SnowStone trees. Two fixes. (1) `LoadMapFromCameraTransition` also re-tints [0,5] whenever natural light differs between the two maps. (2) **Walnut Woods is now `MAP_TYPE_ROUTE`**: it gets real night lighting like its neighbours, and can now be flown out of.

- **3x during Poke Ball throws:** `LaunchBattleAnimation` sets `gWotBallThrowAnimActive` for special anims B_ANIM_BALL_THROW / _WITH_TRAINER, and `WotSkipThisVBlank` keeps fast-forwarding through those, i.e. throw, roll and shakes (every other battle animation still drops to 1x). **Not verified:** a harness v2 that throws 20 balls then a Master Ball through the real bag was written (`scratchpad/wot_ff_repro2.c`) but not run; Joe is testing to save usage.

It matches the warp's **destination** (MAP_RUSTBORO_CITY_GYM), not index 4, so warp renumbering can't break it. The note is also a north-facing bg sign on (41,9): "FROSTWOOD's GYM LEADER is out on PTO." (silent once unlocked).

Cause: a Git Bash heredoc turns `\\n` inside an inline Python script into a real newline. ### 2026-09-14 (round 78) — 3x BATTLE FREEZE FOUND AND FIXED (palette fade); FROSTWOOD BAR + POKéMON CENTER WIRED; FLAG COLLISION FIXED
- **3x freeze after the BAG or POKéMON menu: root cause is a palette-fade bug, NOT DMA starvation.** `ReshowBattleScreenAfterMenu` ends with `BeginHardwarePaletteFade(0xFF, 0, 0x10, 0, 1)`. On the fade's last step `UpdateHardwarePaletteFade` does `hardwareFadeFinishing++` (a **1-bit** field) and, because `shouldResetBlendRegisters`, clears the blend count — which is `gPaletteFade.multipurpose1`, so `sPlttBufferTransferPending` goes 0 and **nothing gates the next call on a VBlank any more**. Only `TransferPlttBuffer` (VBlank) ends a hardware fade, and only if the bit reads 1. At 3x a battle runs **4** logic frames per VBlank: each extra call flipped the bit, an even number of flips per VBlank, so once VBlank saw it clear it saw it clear forever. `gPaletteFade.active` stuck TRUE → `CompleteWhenChoseItem` (and anything else waiting on the fade) hung. That is exactly Joe's screenshot: battle screen rebuilt, colours restored, empty textbox. **Fix (`src/palette.c`):** once `hardwareFadeFinishing` is set, return ACTIVE without stepping again, so the finishing state is sticky until VBlank ends it. At 1x a VBlank always falls between calls, so behaviour there is unchanged.
- **Proven, not guessed — reproduced headless.** A throwaway git worktree (`J:\wot_ff_repro`, since removed) booted straight into a wild battle with fast forward on and drove the REAL player UI by injecting keys in `ReadKeys` (BAG open/close, then POKéMON → slot 2 → SHIFT), with a watchdog that dumps callbacks/controller funcs/fade/tasks over mGBA debug print and exits via SWI 3. Run under `tools/mgba/mgba-rom-test -l15 -Rr0` (the hydra's arguments). **Same harness: without the fix, frozen on the very first bag close (fade active=1, ctrl0=CompleteWhenChoseItem); with it, 25/25 bag+switch cycles, exit 0.** Harness source + diff saved in the session scratchpad (`wot_ff_repro.c`, `ff_repro_harness.diff`).
- **Round 35's diagnosis ("3x stuck battles = DMA starvation", 8x → 4x) was probably this same bug.** 8x is also even; 4x didn't fix it, it just stayed even. The `main.c` comment still describes DMA starvation; the `gAnimScriptActive` exemption is harmless either way. Field fast forward is 3x (odd flips), so a missed VBlank there resolved on the next one.
- **Real bug I introduced in round 72, fixed:** `FLAG_MUNEN_VILLAGE_BOULDER_2` aliased `FLAG_UNUSED_0x4A8`, but `FLAG_WOT_EXP_SHARE_ON` is a **raw 0x4A8** further up flags.h while `FLAG_UNUSED_0x4A8` still says unused — smashing boulder 2 toggled the party Exp Share (and toggling Exp Share hid/showed the boulder). Boulders 2-5 moved to **0x4C0-0x4C3**, clear of the raw WoT run (0x499+). An old save with boulder 2 smashed will show boulders 2-5 again; harmless. **Lesson: a FLAG_UNUSED name is not proof the value is free — grep the raw hex too.**
- **Frostwood bar (`OldaleTown_Mart`, now a bar on FrostwoodBar).** Joe's object numbers are porymap's 1-based local ids. (5,3) menu card = bg sign → `OldaleTown_Mart_EventScript_MenuCard` (bartender turns east, then the same shop as the clerk; he is walled in behind the bar, which has no counter behaviour). (5,4) bottle = hidden **ITEM_SODA_POP**, `FLAG_HIDDEN_ITEM_FROSTWOOD_BAR_SODA_POP` = 0x4C4 (hidden-item flags must be ≥ 0x1F4: the macro stores flag − FLAG_HIDDEN_ITEMS_START). Object 5 (MAN_4) switch-540/twigs story, 6 (Beach Beauty) "I'm just here for the après.", 8 (MAN_5) too nervous → DM her on Instagram, 9 (Greta) board or ski. Text is Joe's wording.
- **Frostwood Pokémon Center (`OldaleTown_PokemonCenter_1F`).** The nurse at (8,5) was unreachable: the counter front (row 6) had no counter behaviour, so talking from (8,7) hit a blocked empty tile. FrostwoodPC metatile **0x2AE** (the tablecloth front, used only there) set to **MB_COUNTER** (behaviour byte only, layer bits asserted) so the stock nurse script runs with the right object selected. Goons: (1,5) "GTFO my face. You smell like MUK.", (0,5) "Don't you have anything better to do, bud?". Boy (3,3) and girl (11,8) now give gondola → SENNEN beach hints. PC (11,3) = bg sign facing north → `EventScript_PC`; its on/off effects only rewrite the tile when it is a vanilla PC tile (`IsPlayerInFrontOfPC`), so Joe's custom computer is left alone.
- **Build clean (exit 0).** Map/NPC changes not play-tested; the battle fix is verified by the harness only.
- **Uncommitted:** rounds 75-78 plus Joe's porymap edits, and the Jirachi-bob fix (`src/wot_confetti.c`, logged by its own session as the other round 77). **NOT PUSHED.**

### 2026-09-14 (round 77) — SHADOW JIRACHI BOB FIXED: the object check was inverted
- **Bug (spotted in round 76):** `Task_WotObjectBob` (`src/wot_confetti.c`) did `if (!TryGetObjectEventIdByLocalIdAndMap(...)) { DestroyTask; return; }`. That helper returns **TRUE when the object is NOT found** and FALSE on success, so the task killed itself on its first frame whenever Shadow Jirachi existed, and the +/-3px float at TowerTop never ran.
- **Fix:** dropped the `!` (destroy only when the call returns TRUE), added a one-line comment saying so, realigned the continuation lines. The rest of the task is unchanged. Still armed from `TowerTop_OnResume` (`VAR_0x8004 = LOCALID_TOWER_JIRACHI`, `special WotStartObjectBob`).
- **GOTCHA:** `TryGetObjectEventIdByLocalIdAndMap` (`src/event_object_movement.c:1449`) is failure-TRUE. `if (!Try...)` means FOUND. Swept the `wot_*`, `shadow*` and `field_specials.c` sources: this was the only WoT caller.
- **Noticed, NOT changed:** the comment says "one cycle every ~4 seconds", but `tSine += 2` over the 256-entry `gSineTable` is 128 frames, about 2.1 s. Step by 1 for ~4 s, or fix the comment.
- **Build clean (exit 0; wot_confetti.o recompiled, ROM relinked 15:55, 0 warnings). NOT play-tested.**
- **Uncommitted:** this one-file change, on top of round 76's uncommitted work. **NOT PUSHED.**

### 2026-09-13 (round 72) — ball swaps use Joe's tiles; rock at 53,23 scoped, not built; two landmines disarmed

For a bg_event there is no object; it should reuse the check/prompt/field-effect half and finish with `setmetatile` + `DrawWholeMapView` + an OnLoad restore, same shape as the balls. (Using Rock Smash from the PARTY MENU will not work on a tile rock — that path looks for an `OBJ_EVENT_GFX_BREAKABLE_ROCK` in front of the player; pressing A on it will.)

### 2026-09-13 (round 71) — item balls solid and pickable ONCE; starter moves; roof advice
- **THE REPEAT-PICKUP BUG WAS `Std_FindItem` CLOBBERING `VAR_RESULT`.** My pickup scripts guarded on VAR_RESULT after `finditem`, so they bailed before `setflag`/`setmetatile` and the ball never went away. **Its real success flag is `VAR_0x8007`**, copied from `checkitemspace` before the pickup and untouched after. **This repo had already been bitten by it once** — `DistortionWorld_3/scripts.inc` carries a comment saying exactly this and works around it with `checkitem`. All three scripts now guard on VAR_0x8007.
- **The three ball tiles are impassable** (collision 1 in map.bin). The pickup's `setmetatile` passes FALSE for the impassable argument, so the snow it leaves behind is walkable again — solid ball, walkable ground, no repeat.
- **Starter move 3 was Dragon Breath on all three; replaced with a species-appropriate standard move:** GIBLE → `MOVE_BITE`, FRIGIBAX → `MOVE_ICY_WIND`, AXEW → `MOVE_ASSURANCE`. Their signature 4th moves (Thousand Arrows / Glaciate / Dragon Darts) are untouched.
- **Draco's ball pickup sound: `SE_EXIT` → `SE_BALL`.** SE_EXIT is the leaving-a-room stinger; SE_BALL is the Poke Ball handling sound.
- **THE ROOF FIX IS LAYER TYPE, NOT COLLISION OR ELEVATION.** Joe set "collision 5", but **collision is a 2-bit field (0-3)** — the map only holds 0 and 1, so it never took. What he changed was elevation (247 tiles at 5). **Neither controls draw order against the player.** `METATILE_LAYER_TYPE` does, and the meanings are counter-intuitive:
  - **NORMAL (0) = middle + top → the top layer draws ABOVE the player.**
  - **COVERED (1) = bottom + middle → BOTH draw below the player.**
  - SPLIT (2) = bottom + top → top above the player.
  - **373 of the walkable tiles on his map are COVERED**, which is precisely "player draws on top of everything". A roof he should pass behind wants **NORMAL** (or SPLIT) with the roof art on the TOP layer, set in porymap's tileset editor.
- Build clean, exit 0. **Uncommitted**, along with rounds 66-70.

### 2026-09-13 (round 70) — CLARKSON: the coord events had the wrong ELEVATION. Reflective snow declawed
- **THE CLARKSON TRIGGER WAS AN ELEVATION MISMATCH, NOT A POSITION ONE.** Joe had already moved the coord events onto walkable tiles and met both flag conditions, so position was never the problem. `GetCoordEventScriptAtPosition` matches **x, y AND elevation**: `coordEvents[i].elevation == elevation || coordEvents[i].elevation == ELEVATION_TRANSITION`. The three events carried **elevation 3**; the trigger tiles at (34..36, 4) are **elevation 0** and the tiles below them are **elevation 4**. **3 matched neither, so they could never fire, wherever he stood.** Set to **0 (`ELEVATION_TRANSITION` = matches any)**.
  - **When a coord event "just doesn't fire" on a walkable tile, check its elevation field before moving it again.** Porymap defaults it to 3 and it is invisible on the map canvas.
- **THE REFLECTIVE SNOW WAS `gTileset_General`'s WATER METATILES, PAINTED AS GROUND.** 306 tiles of Munen Village used General **176/177/178/186/192/194** (`MB_POND_WATER`) and **209** (`MB_PUDDLE`, 235 of them). **Both are in `MetatileBehavior_IsReflective`**, which is why the snow mirrored the player.
  - **CHANGING gTileset_General WOULD HAVE BROKEN 14 OTHER LAYOUTS** — metatile 209 alone is painted by Routes 2, 117 and 120, Safari Zone, Faraway Island, Southern Island and Tower Top, where it is genuinely water. **A primary tileset is shared; never retune its attributes to suit one map.**
  - Instead `tilesets_raw/build_scripts/declaw_munen_water.py` **CLONES** each offender into `gTileset_SnowStone` with `MB_FOOTPRINTS` and repoints Munen Village's map.bin at the clones, preserving collision and elevation bits. **The clone costs ZERO tiles**: a metatile is eight (tile, palette) entries, and those tiles belong to the PRIMARY, which SnowStone already shares — so it looks identical. SnowStone 400 → 407 metatiles, tiles unchanged. Guarded by a `.declawed` marker.
- **The old woman is snappier**: the warp sound now starts *with* the spin instead of after it, three revolutions instead of two, and she is removed the frame the spin ends. **`walk_in_place_faster_*` is the quickest in-place turn the engine has** — there is no "fastest" variant — so "spin faster" can only mean fewer idle frames around it. No sky lift, as asked.
- Build clean. **Uncommitted**, along with rounds 66-69.

### 2026-09-12 (round 68) — ONE ROOT CAUSE BEHIND FOUR BUGS: the scripts used the OLD map's object ids. Plus snow footprints
- **THE BIG ONE. Object ids are just the POSITION in map.json's `object_events` list**, and the scripts were written against `Munen_village_2`'s ordering. **Mom (1) and VillageKid (5) never came across to the new map, so everything after them shifted**, and one wrong number produced four separate symptoms:
  - `questindicator 7` → Sophocles on the old map, the **OLD LADY** on the new one → the "!" over the wrong NPC.
  - `removeobject 2,3,4` (the three ice balls) → Amaura, Anorith and **DRACO** → the player's chosen ball stayed behind AND the rival was deleted.
  - `removeobject 6` (Old Lady) → the Taillow.
  - `addobject 9` (Draco) → FishermanDad; `addobject 10` (Clarkson) → the Caretaker → **neither ever appeared**.
- **FIXED WITH NAMES, NOT NEW NUMBERS.** All 13 objects now carry a `local_id` in map.json and the scripts use `LOCALID_MUNEN_*` constants; **zero bare numeric object ids remain**. Renumbering by hand would have broken again the next time Joe inserts an object — and he is adding them constantly (this map went 350→400 metatiles and 10→13 objects inside two rounds). Build clean, exit 0, ROM 88.73%.
- **SNOW FOOTPRINTS BUILT, AND THEY NEEDED NO NEW ART.** The sand print is 32x16 and uses exactly **two colours, at palette indices 13 and 14**. A sprite's pic is only index data — the colour comes from the template's `paletteTag` — so **the same pic pointed at a palette whose 13/14 are snow IS the snow print**. `snow_footprints.pal` is a copy of `general_0.pal` with those two entries changed and every other index left alone, because other effects share that pic. Eight touch points: palette, INCGFX, `SpritePalette`, `SpriteTemplate`, `FLDEFFOBJ_SNOW_FOOTPRINTS 45` + pointer, `FLDEFF_SNOW_FOOTPRINTS 81` + script in `data/field_effect_scripts.s`, `FldEff_SnowFootprints()`, and a branch in `DoTracksGroundEffect_Footprints` keyed on `MetatileBehavior_IsFootprints(previousMetatileBehavior)`.
- **THE UNDERWATER BUBBLES HAVE A SECOND CAUSE, IN JOE'S MAP DATA.** Beyond round 67's mis-set behaviour, a full scan of every tile in LAYOUT_MUNEN_VILLAGE resolving each metatile to its behaviour found **182 tiles of `gTileset_General` metatile 209, which carries `MB_SHALLOW_WATER`**, many painted walkable. That produces water ripples underfoot. (Also 69 tiles of General 176-194 = `MB_POND_WATER`, presumably the actual pond.) **Neither is in SnowStone; both are primary metatiles Joe painted.** Left alone — his map data.
- **NO SIGN BEHAVIOUR EXISTS ANYWHERE ON THE MAP NOW**, so the POKeMON MART text on the ice should be gone with the `MB_ICE` fix. Worth knowing for next time: **`MB_POKEMART_SIGN` is 30 and `MB_ICE` is 31 — adjacent**, and the mart text fires on BUMPING a tile (`field_control_avatar.c:582`), not on pressing A.
- Also fixed: the **starter prompt no longer wears Draco's nameplate** — `setspeaker` is sticky and the ice-block scripts set none, so whoever spoke last stayed on the plate; all three now `setspeaker SP_NAME_NONE`. And the **old lady leaves with `SE_EXIT`** and a beat either side of `removeobject`.
- **STILL OPEN, needs Joe:** which graphic the item balls should use (he wants "the blue balls", and a swap to plain snow after pickup — that reads like `setmetatile`, not an object event, so it changes the implementation); Draco picking up his own ball and walking to the player before the battle (needs choreography on the new 59x34 layout); and the roof he can stand on — that is layer type + collision on those metatiles, which is porymap work.
- **Uncommitted**, along with rounds 66-67.

### 2026-09-12 (round 67) — the metatile behaviours were the WRONG CONSTANTS; fixed. And there are no snow footprints in vanilla
- **I USED GREP LINE NUMBERS AS ENUM VALUES.** Round 66 read `MB_ICE` and `MB_FOOTPRINTS` off a `grep -n` of `metatile_behaviors.h` and took the `NN:` prefix as the value. **`MB_ICE` is 31, not 37. `MB_FOOTPRINTS` is 36, not 42.** So the ice got **37 = `MB_THIN_ICE`** and the snow got **42 = `MB_REFLECTION_UNDER_BRIDGE`** — which is precisely what Joe reported: no sliding, and an underwater bubble effect on the snow. **A grep's `NN:` is a line number. Parse the enum.** `fix_snow_behaviours.py` now parses `metatile_behaviors.h` properly and never hardcodes a value.
- Why the ice looked inert rather than broken: **`MB_THIN_ICE` only does anything under the per-map cracked-ice task** (`SetSootopolisGymCrackedIceMetatiles` / `SetIcefallCaveCrackedIceMetatiles` in `field_tasks.c`). No such task runs on Munen Village, so it did nothing at all.
- **THE MASK WAS ALSO WRONG, HARMLESSLY, AND IS WORTH REMEMBERING.** With `metatile_attributes_size=2` the Emerald layout is **`METATILE_ATTR_BEHAVIOR_MASK 0x00FF` (bits 0-7)** and **`METATILE_ATTR_LAYER_MASK 0xF000` (bits 12-15)**. Round 66 used `0x1FF`, which is the **FRLG** 9-bit layout (`sMetatileAttrMasks` vs `sMetatileAttrMasksEmerald` in `fieldmap.c`). Both values fit in 8 bits so nothing was corrupted, but the fix uses 0x00FF.
- **JOE HAS RE-LAYERED METATILES BY HAND SINCE, TO FIX WHAT DRAWS ABOVE THE PLAYER.** The fix writes **only bits 0-7** and **asserts that all 400 metatiles' bits 12-15 are byte-identical before and after**, aborting if not. The tileset is now **400 metatiles** (was 350 last round) — he keeps adding; the ice/snow id lists are all below 350 and still valid.
- Final state: **17 metatiles `MB_ICE` (31)**, **16 `MB_FOOTPRINTS` (36)**, plus the pre-existing `MB_POND_WATER` x2 and `MB_SOUTH_ARROW_WARP` x1 left alone. Build clean, exit 0, ROM 88.73%.
- **THERE ARE NO SNOW FOOTPRINTS IN THE BASE GAME.** `graphics/field_effects/pics/` has exactly: `sand_footprints`, `deep_sand_footprints`, `bike_tire_tracks`, `bug_tracks`, `slither_tracks`, `spot_tracks`. **Nothing snow.** So `MB_FOOTPRINTS` produces the SAND print, which reads tan on snow.
- **What a snow variant would take, if wanted.** The sprite is trivially small — 32x16, and only **two colours**, `D5B46A` and `CD9C52` at indices 13 and 14. But **recolouring the PNG alone does nothing**: the pic is index data and the colour comes from the sprite template's `paletteTag`, which is `FLDEFF_PAL_TAG_GENERAL_0` — **shared with other field effects**, so it cannot just be tinted. A real snow variant needs its own pic + palette + tag + `SpriteTemplate` + `FLDEFFOBJ_`/`FLDEFF_` ids and table entries, then a branch in **`DoTracksGroundEffect_Footprints`** (`event_object_movement.c:10352`), which is the single clean hook — it already selects between sand and deep-sand, so it would select on `previousMetatileBehavior == MB_FOOTPRINTS`. Roughly six files. **Not done; Joe's call.**
- **Uncommitted**, along with round 66.

### 2026-09-12 (round 66) — three item balls in Munen Village; ice is slippery and snow takes tracks
- **Three standard `finditem` item balls**: **TM Dig at (10,20)**, **Sacred Ash at (28,9)**, **Light Ball at (18,5)**. Object event + hide flag + `finditem`, exactly the MunenTunnel pattern. The object itself blocks the tile, so **the ground underneath stays passable and needs no impassable painting**, and the flag makes the ball vanish for good once taken. All three target tiles were checked first: passable, and reachable from at least one neighbour. Build clean, exit 0, ROM 88.73%.
- New flags `FLAG_ITEM_MUNEN_VILLAGE_TM_DIG / _SACRED_ASH / _LIGHT_BALL` on `FLAG_UNUSED_0x468 / 0x470 / 0x472`, picked by diffing declared `FLAG_UNUSED_*` slots against those already aliased (199 were free).
- **A SCRIPT OF MINE REPORTED SUCCESS WITHOUT CHANGING ANYTHING.** The flags edit asserted on `anchor` but then called `s.replace(anchor + "\n", ...)` — and the real line has a trailing `// comment`, so the newline never followed the anchor and the replace was a no-op. The assert passed, the file was rewritten unchanged, and it printed "3 item flags added". Caught only by the **link error** (`undefined reference to FLAG_ITEM_MUNEN_VILLAGE_TM_DIG`). **Assert on the string you are actually going to replace, and re-read the file to confirm the change landed** — which the fix now does.
- **`gTileset_SnowStone`: 17 metatiles are `MB_ICE`, 16 are `MB_FOOTPRINTS`.** Attributes stay consistent at 350/350, and the three metatiles that already carried a behaviour (34, 257, 258) were left alone.

  - `MB_FOOTPRINTS` works despite `metatile_behavior.c` commenting that it "is not used by any metatiles": `GetGroundEffectFlags_Tracks` tests it alongside sand and raises `GROUND_EFFECT_FLAG_SAND`. **The comment describes vanilla's data, not the engine's capability.**

A plain "is it blue" filter returned **84** candidates including windows, lamps and grass; matching the reference colour set returns 17, all genuinely ice. **The threshold is 0.55, not 0.85, deliberately**: the ice EDGE tiles carry a rock or fence border and dip below a strict bar, while nothing else in the tileset uses those two cyans, so the lower bar stays safe. Snow ground is pale + solid + actually walkable in the map, which keeps white building walls out.

### 2026-09-12 (round 64) — the snow scenery's transparent top layer is BACK. My bug, found by Joe
- **28 of SnowStone's snow-scenery metatiles were baked FLAT and are now two-layer again**, so the ground behind them can be changed. Joe hit this mapping the new starter village. `tilesets_raw/build_scripts/restore_snow_masks.py`.
- **THE BUG WAS MINE, IN `build_snow_stone`.** droid779_snow was imported through `flatten_secondary()`, which **composites both layers into one image**, and `add_unique()` then re-derived the split from that flattened result: `opaque = all((-1,-1,-1) not in t for t in sub)` → single layer. **Any source cell whose top layer happened to cover its bottom came out "opaque" and was baked flat.** droid779 draws its scenery — trees, rocks, snowballs, signs, drifts — as a properly masked top layer, and 28 of those lost the mask. **Flattening before re-tiling destroys layer information that cannot be recovered from the result.**
- **THE MAPPING BACK TO THE SOURCE CANNOT BE DONE BY CONTENT.** The build re-quantised every colour into SnowStone's palettes, so nothing renders identically any more — a content match found **0 of 163** flat metatiles. **The placement is deterministic though**: replaying the build as far as `pack()` and reading the Builder's `items` labels (`snow[r,c]`) gives metatile id → droid779 cell exactly. The replay is checked against the known **427 tiles / 240 metatiles** and aborts if it does not reproduce them.
- **TWO WRONG APPROACHES WERE TRIED AND REJECTED ON EVIDENCE, NOT ON TASTE:**
  1. **Colour-keying the ground out.** Cost arithmetic said it fitted (157 new tiles, 134 freed, 33 spare). **Rendering it showed it removed almost nothing**, because the ground I keyed was `gTileset_General`'s snow (2 colours) and the real background is **droid779's own textured snow** — a corner histogram shows four colours at ~12.5% each, no dominant one, and those same pale colours appear in the scenery itself. **A tile budget that fits is not evidence the transform is right.**
  2. **Reading the split from `gTileset_SnowAndStone` for the buildings.** Its built metatiles are 93% two-layer, which looked like the same recoverable regression — but **the buildings did not come from that tileset at all**, they came from the raw artwork PNG with the checkerboard keyed out. Indexing the built metatiles by sheet cell produced a meaningless "169 of 169". **Check which SOURCE a group was actually built from before reading structure off a namesake.**
- **THE BUILDINGS ARE CORRECTLY FLAT AND NEED NO FIX.** For them `opaque` meant "this raw cell had no checkerboard behind it" — solid building face — so flat is right and there is nothing to mask.
- **JOE HAD HAND-EDITED THE TILESET AND THE GUARD CAUGHT IT.** `.generated` refused a rebuild, naming 15 changed files. He had added **83 metatiles (267-349)** in porymap; `tiles.png` was **untouched** and the palettes were re-saved byte-identically. **The guard was NOT re-stamped afterwards, deliberately** — leaving it tripped is what keeps `build_snow_stone` from ever overwriting his metatiles. A `.masks` marker makes the re-mask itself idempotent.
- **NOTHING OF HIS MOVED, AND THAT WAS PROVEN, NOT ASSUMED.** Verified against a pre-edit backup: metatile count unchanged at 350; **exactly the 28 target metatiles changed and no others**; Joe's 267-349 byte-identical; 61 tile slots overwritten and **zero of them referenced by any surviving metatile**. New tiles went only into slots nothing referenced after the conversion — **a slot can be repurposed in place; only REMOVING one shifts the rest.**
- **62 new top-layer tiles**, ported into SnowStone's existing palettes by picking the best-fitting slot per tile (no palette changes). Tile slots stay at 496, metatiles at 350. The new bottom layer is General's snow metatile 94, matching what the cells that kept their mask already use.
- **9 of the 121 metatiles MUNEN_VILLAGE paints are among the 28.** They render the same except a few that sat on droid779's grass and now sit on snow — which is the point: the ground is Joe's to choose now.
- **Uncommitted.**

Game repo: `data/tilesets/**/.generated`.

### 2026-09-12 (round 61) — `LOCALID_RIVALS_HOUSE_1F_MOM` split in two: the redefinition warning is gone, and so is a real misfire
- **The duplicate `local_id` round 60 spotted is fixed at the source, in `map.json`, not in the generated header.** `LOCALID_RIVALS_HOUSE_1F_MOM` is now **`LOCALID_BRENDANS_HOUSE_1F_MOM` (2)** in `LittlerootTown_BrendansHouse_1F` and **`LOCALID_MAYS_HOUSE_1F_MOM` (1)** in `LittlerootTown_MaysHouse_1F`. Build **exit 0**, ROM **88.71%**, EWRAM **229788 B** and IWRAM **28596 B** — all three identical to round 60 — and the **~20 copies of the redefinition warning are at zero**.
- **IT WAS NOT JUST NOISE: BRENDAN'S HOUSE WAS ANIMATING THE WRONG WOMAN.** `map_event_ids.h` is one flat namespace, May's `#define ... 1` came after Brendan's `... 2`, so the later one won everywhere. In Brendan's house local id 1 is **`LOCALID_PLAYERS_HOUSE_1F_MOM` (`OBJ_EVENT_GFX_MOM`)** — a second, simultaneously-present NPC in that same room — so `LittlerootTown_BrendansHouse_1F_EventScript_YoureNewNeighbor` hung the "!", the 48-frame delay and the whole `RivalMomApproach` walk on **the player's mom** instead of the rival's mom (`OBJ_EVENT_GFX_WOMAN_4`, local 2). May's house was resolving to its own object by luck, not by design.
- **BOTH names were changed, not just one, and that is the point.** Renaming only the loser leaves `LOCALID_RIVALS_HOUSE_1F_MOM` in the header still meaning "May's mom" — a name that reads as either house and resolves to one. The ambiguous name now exists nowhere, so a future script cannot pick it up by accident.
- **The fix site is `data/maps/*/map.json`; the header says "DO NOT MODIFY" and means it.** `map_data_rules.mk:41-42` regenerates `include/constants/map_event_ids.h` with `mapjson event_constants` from every `MAP_JSONS`, and it sits in `AUTO_GEN_TARGETS` — so editing the JSON is the whole job, and `make` rewrote the header itself.
- **Only a macro redefined with a DIFFERENT value warns.** `LOCALID_PLAYERS_HOUSE_1F_MOM` is *also* defined twice — Brendan's house and `PalletTown_PlayersHouse_1F_Frlg` — but both are `1`, which C treats as a benign redefinition and passes silently. `LOCALID_RIVALS_HOUSE_1F_MOM` was the **only** genuinely conflicting macro in the entire header; a sort-and-count over the defines now reports **none**.
- Full reference sweep before touching anything: **8 references, all inside the two map directories** (each map's `map.json`, plus three `applymovement`s in its own `scripts.inc`). Nothing in `src/`, `data/scripts/` or `asm/`. The shared `RivalsHouse_1F_EventScript_RivalMom::` that **both** moms' objects point at lives in `MaysHouse_1F/scripts.inc:112` and references no local id at all — which is why it needed no edit.
- **PORYMAP WAS OPEN THE WHOLE TIME (PID 1816) AND STILL IS.** The map.json mtimes are the edit's own (12:36:51), so it has not written since — but its in-memory model still holds the OLD name. **Close porymap without saving, or reload the project, before touching either Littleroot house**, or a save will put the collision straight back. Brendan's `map.json` also still carries the uncommitted Sep-10 porymap work (the x-shifts and the `MAP_MUNEN_VILLAGE_2` warps); the rename sits alongside it, untouched.
- **THE TWO REMAINING BUILD WARNINGS ARE BOTH PRE-EXISTING; NEITHER IS FROM THIS ROUND.** (1) Four `src/data/items.h:14834-14837` "missing terminating `"` character" on `ITEM_YACHT_KEY` — its `COMPOUND_STRING` has **real newlines baked into the string literals** instead of `\n`, i.e. **the python-generator backslash bug this file already logs twice**; spawned as its own task. (2) One assembler `explicit waitstate follows implicit waitstate` from `data/maps/CelebiIsland/scripts.inc:121`.
- **FOUND, NOT FIXED: THE SAME LEAK, WITH NO WARNING TO CATCH IT.** May's house holds only **2** objects (mom = 1, ninja boy = 2), yet its `scripts.inc` still drives **`LOCALID_PLAYERS_HOUSE_1F_MOM`** (= 1, defined by *Brendan's* house — in May's house that id is the rival's mom, so the `setobjectxyperm`/`setobjectmovementtype` at lines 33-44 move **her**) and **`LOCALID_RIVALS_HOUSE_1F_RIVAL`** (= 3, defined only by Brendan's house — **May's house has no local id 3**, so `addobject` at line 176 spawns nothing and the `MayApproachPlayer`/`MayGoUpstairs` movements after it have no object to move). The values don't conflict, so **the compiler will never warn about either**. Most likely dead vanilla scene text left behind when the map was rewired to Munen Village — but if that scene is meant to be live, it is broken. Joe's map data; untouched.
