### 2026-08-26 (round 3) — chart toggle actually reachable + CELEBI ISLAND SOFTLOCK FIX (BUILT green 19:27, untested)
- **Why R did nothing (root cause):** `WotPostgameMapActive()` requires FLAG_WOT_POSTGAME_MAP_GET, which is only set during Nessa's Zoroark-reveal beat -- a ONE-SHOT scene that had already played on Joe's save, so the flag could never be granted. Added the same retroactive migration used for the Shadow Log, in `CB2_ContinueSavedGame` (src/overworld.c): `FLAG_WOT_HIDEOUT_REVEAL_DONE && !FLAG_WOT_POSTGAME_MAP_GET -> set it`. **Any existing save gets the chart on next load.** (Secondary factor: the fly map is only reachable once FLY is unlocked, i.e. post-credits.)
- **Toggle now works on the REGION MAP too, not just the fly map.** R is already taken in `field_region_map.c` by the Poke Rider fly shortcut, so the region-map toggle is **SELECT**; the fly map accepts **either R or SELECT**. Both screens open the map with a NULL BgTemplate (bgManaged FALSE), so the in-place chart swap's direct VRAM writes are valid in both. New `WotPostgameMapActive_CanToggle()` (= "chart owned") gates both, separate from `WotPostgameMapActive()` (= "chart currently shown").
- **CELEBI ISLAND SOFTLOCK — real map-geometry trap, now fixed.** The trio trial was reachable ONLY from coord triggers at y=29, which sit in a pocket sealed by a solid wall across y=30-33; the only opening in the wall north of the trio is the single tile (25,27), and stepping from there to y=28 is blocked by the guardian objects themselves. So the trigger row was **physically unreachable** and the only possible action -- talking to a guardian -- always ran `CelebiIsland_Text_GuardianBlocks`. Progress was impossible. Fixes:
  1. `CelebiIsland_EventScript_Guardian` now checks the three keepers (HANNAH/SAMANTHA/MAURA) and, once all are defeated, jumps straight into the yield/scatter/CELEBI-descent scene; otherwise it shows the old blocking line. **Talking to them is now the intended path, exactly as Joe described.**
  2. The trial body was split into `CelebiIsland_EventScript_TrioYield` so both the coord trigger and the interaction reach the same scene.
  3. Added a coord trigger at **(25,27)** -- the one walkable approach tile -- so simply walking up to them also fires it.
  - The old y=29 triggers are left in place: harmless, and they still work if the layout ever opens up.
- TEST CHECKLIST: load an existing save -> chart granted; SELECT toggles on the town-map screen; R or SELECT toggles on the fly map; markers rebuild on both; on Celebi Island beat all three keepers then walk up to / talk to a guardian -> scatter + Celebi descends.

Five rows added with `.regionMapType = REGION_MAP_WOT_POSTGAME` and `.flag = FLAG_WOT_POSTGAME_MAP_GET`, and the markers appear automatically. Tile budget stays 250/256.

Fuji removed** from this chart (they live on the main survey).

Its MAIN-chart coordinates deliberately duplicate Shin-Tokyo's (16,4) so the player icon still sits correctly on the main survey while inside the hideout; the post-game override moves it to (12,7). `data/maps/RebelHideout/map.json` repointed. ### 2026-08-26 — v1.2.0: dragon starters + POST-GAME REGION MAP (BUILT green 17:56, untested)
- **STARTER TRIO -> GIBLE / FRIGIBAX / AXEW.** (Note: the trio actually in the game was PAWNIARD/IMPIDIMP/DEINO -- the script labels IceTyrunt/IceAmaura/IceAnorith are legacy names from an even older trio, which is why "Amaura" looked like a starter. All three are replaced.) Slot 0 Gible, 1 Frigibax, 2 Axew, all Lv5 with hand-picked movesets; the three ice-block flavour texts retuned to the new species.
- **Starter picture at the decision point uses vanilla `showmonpic SPECIES, 10, 3` / `hidemonpic`** -- the same command the Birch lab uses for its own three-way pick. An earlier attempt to add Gible/Frigibax/Axew art to the custom PORTRAIT system was REVERTED: showmonpic needs zero new art, zero C, and shows the real species graphic (~2KB ROM saved per mon). Portraits remain for humans only.
- **RIVAL COUNTER-PICKS MIGRATED (this was the real hazard).** Draco carries the starter one slot before yours, across THREE fights, and the parties were hard-coded to the OLD trio's lines. All-Dragon starters vs the old Dark/Fairy rival would have made Dragon Breath deal ZERO damage vs Impidimp/Morgrem/Grimmsnarl. Migrated: HAS_AMAURA Deino->Axew, TORII_AURORUS Zweilous->Fraxure, LAB_AURORUS Hydreigon->Haxorus; HAS_ANORITH Pawniard->Gible, TORII_ARMALDO Bisharp->Gabite, LAB_ARMALDO Kingambit->Garchomp; HAS_TYRUNT Impidimp->Frigibax, TORII_TYRANTRUM Morgrem->Arctibax, LAB_TYRANTRUM Grimmsnarl->Baxcalibur.
- **POST-GAME REGION MAP — format cracked (document this, it is not obvious):** the region map background is **8bpp, a 64x64 AFFINE tilemap with ONE BYTE per entry** (no flip/palette bits) => a hard ceiling of **256 unique tiles**; the palette is **48 colours** loaded to BG slots 7/8/9, so tile pixel indices must be **112..159**. `map.bin` is 4096 bytes. The main chart uses 240 tiles. Decoding it as a normal 16-bit tilemap produces garbage -- that is the trap.
  - `graphics/pokenav/region_map/map_postgame.{png,bin,pal}` generated from `region map/post game map.jpg`: resized to 28x15 tiles (224x120), median-filtered, quantised to 48 colours, tiles deduplicated then **clustered rarest-into-nearest down to 250**. Content laid into the 64x64 grid at offset (MAPCURSOR_X_MIN, MAPCURSOR_Y_MIN) = (1,2) so **layout[y][x] sits exactly under cursor (x+1, y+2)**.
  - New `REGION_MAP_WOT_POSTGAME` in gRegionMapInfos (dex fields deliberately alias Hoenn's -- the Pokedex never resolves to this chart).
  - New layout `src/data/region_map/region_map_layout_wot_postgame.h`.
  - **`gRegionMapEntries[]` stores ONE coordinate per mapsec globally** -- the blocker for a second chart of the same region. Solved with `WotMapSecEntry()`, an accessor that returns a post-game override (`sWotPostgameEntries[]`, width==0 = fall through) and now backs every cursor/icon placement read in region_map.c.
  - `GetRegionMapType()` is deliberately NOT modified (the Pokedex area screen shares it); region-map graphics selection goes through the new `WotResolveRegionMapType()` instead.
- **TOGGLE = R on the fly map.** `MAP_INPUT_R_BUTTON` was already emitted but unhandled there (R is taken in field_region_map for Poke Rider fly). `WotToggleRegionMapChart()` flips FLAG_WOT_POSTGAME_MAP_ACTIVE, re-sends only tiles/tilemap/palette (safe: both callers pass a NULL BgTemplate so bgManaged is FALSE = direct VRAM writes), then re-derives the cursor's mapsec because the same screen position means a different place on the other chart.
- **Nessa hands it over on her own reveal beat** in RebelHideout, immediately after the Zoroark illusion drops and before the defector's briefing -- sets **FLAG_WOT_POSTGAME_MAP_GET 0x49B** (gates the toggle); **FLAG_WOT_POSTGAME_MAP_ACTIVE 0x49C** is the live toggle state.
- **NEEDS JOE'S CONFIRMATION — the location placements are my reading of the art, not verified against canon:** Ryuden Island = the torii island (N), Shin-Tokyo = the neon city (centre), Celebi Island = the heart-shaped pink island (E), Prototype Island = the small NE islet, Ashlands = the arid island (W), Mt. Fuji = the southern mountain wall, with Routes 129/130 in the open water. Fixing any of these is a one-line edit in the layout header + its `sWotPostgameEntries` row.
- Escape-level `
` corruption in generated `.string` lines bit AGAIN (5th time) -- fixed with the Edit tool. Rule stands: never write `.string` content through a bash-heredoc python script.
- TEST CHECKLIST: pick each starter (art shows, correct mon+moves); each rival fight fields the right dragon at Lv5/21/40; Nessa's chart gift fires once at the reveal; R toggles the chart in the fly map both ways; cursor names and fly destinations correct on BOTH charts; Pokedex area screen unaffected.

### 2026-08-12 — custom battle backdrops, shadow sprite grounding, surf-exit fix (BUILT green, untested)
- **Surf south from Shin-Tokyo** now lands at Route129 **(37,1)** (was 26,1 = outside the playable water; verified (37,1) open channel via collision decode).

Wave-3 sprites were already aligned. Fix: shift art up to the vanilla anim_front frame-0 bbox bottom; 10 species with no headroom were shrunk ≤11% and bottom-aligned (incl. **jirachi 0.89** — check the endgame battle reads OK).
  - **PIPELINE BUG THAT HID THIS: PIL `.point()` on a P-mode image remaps indices but `.convert('L')` then reads PALETTE colors — the mask is garbage and every bbox comes back full-canvas.** My first audit reported 0 misaligned because of this. Mask P images with a manual pixel loop, always.

  - **WOT_INDOORS** (FR patch) — TradewindGym. **WOT_TOWN** — MAPSEC_CELEBI_ISLAND. **WOT_LAB** — Shadow Deoxys (SPECIES_DEOXYS case in the legendary switch), THE ONI (TRAINER_WALLY_VR_5), Draco/Armored Mewtwo (TRAINER_WALLY_VR_4). **SPACE** — filled the empty vanilla BATTLE_ENVIRONMENT_SPACE slot with the FR starfield; DragonKeeper duo (TRAINER_AMY_AND_LIV_3 two_trainers battle).

### 2026-08-11 (round 8) — PLAY-TESTED + history rewrite + public-readiness pass
- **ALL POST-GAME CONTENT PLAY-TESTED AND VERIFIED BY JOE.** Rounds 4-6 (Nessa's welcome and the Private Jet, the FLY jet animation, the gathered rebellion and mugshots, the liberated Shin-Tokyo, Draco's call, the Armored Mewtwo battle and handover, the recut credits, the Coin Case, the 15 Snag Balls, the quest closures) are **no longer "untested"** — treat them as shipped and working. Earlier rounds in this log still carry their original "untested" wording; round 8 supersedes it for all post-game items.
- **HISTORY REWRITTEN on the workspace `main` branch** with `git filter-repo --path Tools --path mGBA-0.10.5-win32 --invert-paths --refs main`, then force-pushed. Content reachable from `main` went **~786 MB → 87 MB**. Verified after: all 6 commits present, 0 Tools/mGBA objects reachable, gitlinks intact, working-tree files untouched on disk.
  - **`--refs main` was essential**: this clone also holds remote-tracking refs for the GAME repo (`origin/master`, `origin/feature/*`) because both repos share one GitHub remote. An unscoped filter-repo would have rewritten or migrated those. The force push was likewise scoped (`git push --force origin main:main`) — never `--all` or `--mirror`, which would have destroyed the game branches.
  - A server-side backup ref (`backup/main-pre-filter-repo`) was created first — free, since it only pointed at objects already on the server — and deleted after verification because it would otherwise have pinned the 699 MB. Pre-rewrite tip was `363777c053` if it is ever needed from a local reflog.
  - **GitHub's reported repo size will not drop immediately**; unreferenced objects persist until GitHub's own gc runs. Nothing further to do locally.
- **`.gitmodules` ADDED — this was a real bug.** The workspace carried five gitlink entries (`pokeemerald-expansion` — the game itself — plus `agbcc`, `pokefirered`, `pokemonHnS`, `Team-Aquas-Asset-Repo`) with **no `.gitmodules` at all**, so a fresh public clone got five empty directories and `git submodule update --init` had nothing to resolve. URLs recovered from each checkout's own origin remote.
- **Game repo root tidied:** five unreferenced `_nature_build*.py` scratch scripts (620 lines, all five iterations of the same `mt_moon_village_interior` tileset) → the final one is now `tools/build_mt_moon_village_interior.py` beside its twelve siblings; the four superseded drafts removed. Also dropped `_pre_nature_backup/` (19 tracked files) from inside that shipped tileset directory. **Build verified green afterwards (ROM 22:48).**
- **Still open before going public:** (1) the PR for `feature/armored-mewtwo` — needs one interactive `gh auth login`; (2) **decide whether the workspace `main` branch should be public at all** — it contains the GDD, `WoT_Act3_Canon.md`, and every handoff doc, i.e. complete story spoilers. The game repo (`master`) contains no such material.

- **GitHub CLI note:** the real gh (v2.97.0) is at `C:\Program Files\GitHub CLI\gh.exe`, but an Anaconda script named `gh` **shadows it on PATH** — a bare `gh` runs the wrong tool. gh also has no auth (`hosts.yml` absent, no `GH_TOKEN`); plain `git` auth works via credential-manager, which is why pushes succeed but `gh pr create` does not.
- **Corrected an earlier claim in this log's round-5 discussion:** the large Porytiles files are 72–93 MB, *under* GitHub's 100 MB hard limit — they trigger the 50 MB warning only. An earlier statement that the push would be rejected outright was wrong; both pushes succeeded.

### 2026-08-11 (round 6) — jet-spawn root cause, quote strip, portrait/credits trims (BUILT green 19:01, untested)
- **JET SPAWN BUG — ROOT CAUSE (worth remembering):** Joe saw the jet already parked on the green crate, then fly LEFT past it. Two compounding causes, both fixed:
  1. **Flags default to CLEAR, so "hide" flags hide nothing until something SETS them.** FLAG_WOT_HIDE_POSTGAME_JET was only set in the pre-clear ON_TRANSITION branch — a save that was already game-cleared before the jet existed never runs that branch, so the jet spawned visible from its map.json template at (8,3). Now the post-clear branch explicitly `setflag`s it whenever the welcome scene hasn't played (`call_if_unset ... _HideJet`). **Rule: any hide-flag whose "hidden" state must survive a mid-development save needs setting on the SHOWN path too, not just the hidden one.**
  2. **`setobjectxyperm` edits only the TEMPLATE, and `addobject` is a no-op on an already-spawned object.** With the jet already live at (8,3), the reposition to (17,3) was silently ignored and the 9-tile leftward swoop ran from the crate. The scene now `removeobject`s first, then clearflag → setobjectxyperm → addobject.
- Verified in engine source: `TrySpawnObjectEventTemplate`/`TrySetupObjectEventSprite` do **no bounds check** on template x/y, so the off-map (17,3) spawn is legitimate (SennenAct2's jet at x=34 and TowerTop's at x=15/16-wide are the precedents).
- **Nessa's post-game dialogue de-quoted** (all four texts: congrats / on-the-house / almost-forgot / FLY pitch). Her older Act-3 lines still use quotes — say the word if those should match.
- **MOM + MASTER GEN portraits removed** (no art exists): their speaker→portrait rows are gone and PORTRAIT_COUNT is back to 13, so they show the nameplate alone. `WotShowSpeakerPortrait` hides any auto portrait on a miss, so unbound = clean nameplate. Comment left in sSpeakerPortraits saying exactly where to add a row when art lands.
- **Credits pics: +Ghost, +Hellraiser** (converted from `custom sprites/ghost.png` and ToxShadows642's Hellraiser — thumbnail cropped off the source sheet first; 14 and 15 colours). **The interlude budget is a hard 12** (4 interludes × 3 parked pics), so two of MY filler picks were swapped out to make room: **shadow dragonite + shadow alakazam are OUT**; Joe's seven originals, Charizard, Lugia, Ghost, Hellraiser and the Flatfoot logo are the 12. New order: jirachi/mewtwo/gengar | oni/officer/teddiursa | absol/ghost/hellraiser | charizard/lugia/flatfoot. Hellraiser's artist (ToxShadows642) is already on the Character Art page.
- TEST CHECKLIST (this round): jet absent at spawn → swoops in from the right edge → lands on the green crate → still there on re-entry; Nessa's lines quote-free; Mom/Master Gen nameplate-only; credits show Ghost + Hellraiser in interlude 3.

### 2026-08-11 (post-merge) — SHADOW LOG + summary-screen shadow badge (BUILT green 12:24, untested)
- **Joe's question answered: the Shadow Log needs NO retroactive Pokedex work.** It tracks independently of the dex (which was never maintained): per-species bits set at the moment a Shadow enters the player's possession, plus a **Continue-time backfill** that scans party + all PC boxes — a mon that IS_SHADOW proves a snag; a mon wearing the **NATIONAL RIBBON** (only the shrine grants it, set by PurifyPartyMon) proves snag + purification. Old saves self-heal on every Continue; the scan is idempotent.
- **New module `src/wot_shadow_log.c`:** fixed ledger of **45 species** (Celebi Island wilds 5, Route 129 water/rods 8, patrol pools 9+8, story-trainer snag targets 13, Deoxys + Jirachi). Storage = var bitfields (**VAR_WOT_SHADOWLOG_SNAG_0-3 / _PURE_0-3**, renamed from 8 unused vars — 64-species headroom, save-compatible). **APPEND-ONLY: new species go at the END of sWotShadowLogSpecies[] or old saves' bits shift.**
- **Hooks (3):** `BS_WotGiveSnaggedMon` (trainer snags), `Cmd_givecaughtmon` GIVE state (wild shadows + scripted legendaries), `PurifyPartyMon` (purified). Backfill runs in `CB2_ContinueSavedGame` beside the other WoT migrations.
- **UI: START → LOG** (appears once the first Shadow is on the books) — HMs-menu pattern: start-menu callback runs `WotShadowLog_EventScript_Open` (data/scripts/wot_shadow_log.inc) → `special WotShowShadowLog` → header window ("SHADOW LOG  SNAGGED x/45  PURE y") + a **7-row scrollable ListMenu** over the live field (debug-menu window pattern, BG0 pal 15, baseBlock 1). Unsnagged rows show "????????"; snagged rows show the species name + SNAGGED/PURIFIED. A or B closes. Remember the start menu's **no-fade exemption list** — WotShadowLogCallback added beside WotHMsCallback (miss it and the screen fades under the script).
- **Summary-screen shadow badge (Joe):** 8×16 two-frame sheet `graphics/summary_screen/wot_shadow_state.png` (frame 0 = the battle healthbox shadow flame, frame 1 = purified light, merged 6-colour palette, tag 0x4B50) at **(14,46)** — tucked against the portrait's top-left on every summary page. Refreshes on mon switch (both LoadMonGfxAndSprite sites), hidden for ordinary mons, destroyed in BeginCloseSummaryScreen. GOTCHA: **EWRAM_DATA statics must zero-init** — the sprite-id holder uses 0=none/id+1 encoding because `= SPRITE_NONE` fails the build.

All crossings are coord-trigger + script-warp (plain warps never fire on water behaviours) and every landing tile was behaviour-verified as MB_OCEAN_WATER/MB_DEEP_WATER before wiring.
- **BUG FOUND: PrototypeIsland's south surf row was at y=28 on a 28-tall map — out of bounds**, so surfing south did nothing at all. Moved to **y=27** (22 triggers) and retargeted from the stale `(1,36)` to **Celebi Island (25,1)**, its north water.
- **New links:** Prototype **north** y=0 (22 triggers) → Route130 (42,31) · Route130 **south** y=32, x28-57 (30) → Prototype (11,1) · Route130 **north** y=0, x3-17 (15) → **Ryuden Island / DragonKeeper (20,33)** · DragonKeeper **south** y=34 (35 triggers, the 4 egg triggers preserved) → Route130 (10,1). Route130's vanilla Hoenn connections cleared; its Mirage Island `OnTransition` left intact.

- **Route 130 wilds = Route 129's** (deep-copied through the JSON parser, `base_label` gRoute130): water rate 4 — Squirtle/Lanturn/Mudkip/Piplup/Politoed Lv30-38; fishing rate 30 — old rod Squirtle/Mudkip, good rod Lanturn/Piplup/Politoed, super rod Kingdra/Dragonair/Empoleon Lv36-42. **NOT shadow:** the `CreateWildMon` shadow condition still covers only MAPSEC_CELEBI_ISLAND + MAPSEC_ROUTE_129 — say the word and MAPSEC_ROUTE_130 is a one-line addition.

- **Credits trimmed to 19 pages** per Joe: **Joe Maloney appears exactly ONCE**, on the last page as "A game by / Joe Maloney" (the Created-by / Story / Game Design / Map Design / Scripting pages are gone); **Character Art = ToxShadows642 only** (Rafael Sanna stays on Sprite Design); **Playtesting is ONE page** with Luke Devereux + Mike Mancuso + Kyle Clarkson together (the three solo QA pages deleted); closing is now **"Thank you for playing." → "A game by / Joe Maloney"**; new **"Flatfoot Games"** studio page before the closing.

Sources: the shadow sheets are copies of `graphics/pokemon_shadow/*.png`; the two villains are copies of `graphics/trainers/front_pics/mutrid_leader.png` / `mutrid_grunt_white.png`; armored mewtwo is the front half of `custom sprites/Armored Mewtwo/armored_mewtwo_front_back.png` (green-keyed); white gengar is the front frame of `custom sprites/shiny gengar.png`; the logo is `Claude Handoff/rom_assets/intro/flatfoot_emblem.png` scaled to 44×64.

- **GOTCHA: `graphics/intro_flatfoot/flatfoot_overlay.png` is a raw TILE SHEET**, not the assembled logo (scaling it produced garbage) — the assembled source art lives in `Claude Handoff/rom_assets/intro/flatfoot_logo.png` (184×50 wordmark) and `flatfoot_emblem.png` (34×50 mark). The wordmark shrinks to an illegible 17px tall in a 64px slide, so the **emblem** is what shows; the wordmark is carried by the "Flatfoot Games" text page.

### 2026-08-11 (later) — CRASH FIX, single Rayquaza, Jirachi bob, CUSTOM CREDITS (BUILT green 10:41, untested)
- **CONFETTI CRASH FIXED (Joe: "crashed after the confetti due to too many sprites").** My spawner was uncapped: 2 chips every 3 frames for 180 frames, each living ~80-160 frames → ~60-100 live sprites against the shared **64-sprite pool**, starving the field's own object events (player included) and taking the GameClear handoff down with it. Now **hard-capped at 18 live** (counter incremented on spawn, decremented when a chip falls off screen, reset on start/stop) at 1 chip every 4 frames. Still reads as a downpour. **RULE: any field sprite spawner MUST cap against MAX_SPRITES — the field's objects share that pool.**
- **Legendaries → ONE Rayquaza (Joe).** The "missing pixels" were clipping: `OBJ_EVENT_GFX_SPECIES(...)` mon objects are 32×32, but Groudon/Kyogre/Rayquaza art is 64×64, so a quarter of each rendered. Switched to **`OBJ_EVENT_GFX_RAYQUAZA_STILL` (41)** — the real 64×64 cutscene sprite whose `sPicTable_RayquazaStill` has all **9 frames** on `sAnimTable_Standard`, so it is both complete art AND safe under `applymovement` (unlike `OBJ_EVENT_GFX_RAYQUAZA` 207, which is 64×64 on the 5-frame `sAnimTable_Rayquaza` — walk actions read past that table). Groudon/Kyogre objects + their scatter movements deleted; TowerTop is back to 4 objects (1 Jirachi · 2 Oni · 3 jet · **4 Rayquaza at (8,6)**) and the `.equ` block matches. Rayquaza bursts out with its cry and climbs straight up out of the tower.
- **Shadow Jirachi now bobs:** new special **`WotStartObjectBob`** (VAR_0x8004 = local id, in `src/wot_confetti.c`): a task drives `sprite->y2` off `gSineTable` for a ±3px float, self-destructs the moment that object stops existing. Hooked on **MAP_SCRIPT_ON_RESUME** (not ON_FRAME) so the float also resumes after a battle return; re-arming while it runs is a no-op. Reusable for any floating/hovering object.
- **CUSTOM CREDITS — `src/data/credits.h` rewritten wholesale** (engine untouched; it just walks PAGE_COUNT × 5 lines, changing scene every PAGE_COUNT/9 pages). **27 pages → PAGE_INTERVAL 3.** Roll: title → **Created by / Story & Direction / Game Design: Joe Maloney** → **Sprite Design: Rafael Sanna, aveontrainer, baylorhernandez, biadoxaf, DarkusShadow, jaov46** → **Character Art: Rafael Sanna, ToxShadows642** → **Tileset Art: pinkscales, Phyromatical, MagiScarf, PeekyChew, Elinthind, lo8jd, Dark Slayer** → **Shin-Tokyo Art: Emeiry / Odisea by ekat99** (honors the CC BY-NC-SA obligation) → Map Design + Event Scripting: Joe Maloney → Music → **Playtesting: Luke Devereux, Mike Mancuso, Kyle Clarkson** (then a solo QA page each) → Built With pokeemerald-expansion / Rom Hacking Hideout / pret decomp → Based on POKéMON EMERALD, GAME FREAK · Nintendo → Special Thanks → "…and to you / for playing." → "Every wish is its own again." → **Wishes of Tomorrow / Joe Maloney**. All lines ≤30 chars (the credits window overruns past that). Artist handles were recovered from the `by_<artist>` DeviantArt filenames in `tilesets_raw/` and `custom sprites/`.

### 2026-08-07 (final pass) — AMBUSH REORDERED per Joe + rumble landing + card duck (BUILT green 15:14, untested)
- **NEW ORDER (Joe's revision): land → THEME DETONATES → stand + walk → dialogue → CARD → battle.** The card is now visual-only for Edwards.
- **Landing sound restored to the looping SE_M_EARTHQUAKE rumble Joe liked** — now with a deliberate cut: new special **`WotStopSceneSE`** (sound.c; m4aMPlayStop on SE1+SE2, never the ambient SE3) ends the loop after ~1.5s of rumble UNDER the erupting theme. This is THE way to end any looping field SE on cue.
- **New special `WotPrimeBossTheme`** (boss_intro.c; song id in VAR_0x8004): public `WotStartBossTheme(song)` = the ResetMapMusic → m4aMPlayStop(BGM) → m4aSongNumStart → fadeOI=0 → **gWotBossIntroPrimed=TRUE** block, callable mid-scene. The primed flag now lives from the LANDING through dialogue and card to the battle (transition + no-jingle gating unchanged).
- **Card behavior when already primed:** INIT skips FadeOutMapMusic and instead **DUCKS the running theme (m4aMPlayVolumeControl → 100/256)** so the slam whoosh is guaranteed to ring over it; slam skips the tMusicWait restart; DONE restores volume 256 (safe no-op otherwise). Unprimed bosses (Allison) behave exactly as before.
- Scene beats: quake loop + 9px shake → waitstate → theme + delay 60 → WotStopSceneSE → camera home → jet cleanup → crouch→stand swap → walk + face → KannonIntro (theme under) → bossintro (duck + slam) → battle.
- **THE ACTUAL ROOT CAUSE (round 3, BUILT green 14:55): m4a MaxChn was vanilla's 5.** The whole sound engine allowed FIVE simultaneous DirectSound channels; the ported DP/BW themes saturate 5 alone, so every field SE (whistle, boom, card slam) lost the channel fight no matter what got muted. `src/m4a.c` init now runs **12 channels (the mixer's max)** — SEs + dense music + ambience coexist engine-wide. This likely also improves audio mush everywhere else in the hack (battles under DP themes etc.). Storm mute + silent shake retained (still correct: cleaner mix, storm still auto-returns). Shake raised again per Joe: **vertical 9 / horizontal 3 / 10 shakes / delay 3.**

The boom cutting the whistle at ground contact is the natural read.
- **CARD AUDIO REBUILT — the "banner sound muted partway" bug:** TWO stacked causes on one SE player: (1) the frame-0 cue SE (SE_M_DETECT) was replaced mid-ring by the slam PlaySE; (2) the theme's dense opening chord could steal m4a channels from the slam whoosh. Card now has **exactly ONE sound** — the slam SE at banner arrival — and the battle theme starts **10 frames after the slam** (`tMusicWait`, reads as "on the slam") via new helper `BossIntro_StartBattleTheme` (ResetMapMusic → m4aMPlayStop(BGM only) → m4aSongNumStart → fadeOI=0 → primed). **A/B-skip guard:** skipping inside the 10-frame window fires the theme+primed flag immediately — the primed flag must NEVER be lost or the battle plays encounter music + wrong transition. `seCue` struct field retired (kept in data, unused). RULES: one SE player — never PlaySE while an earlier scene SE must finish; SE_M_EARTHQUAKE loops on the field; SE-vs-BGM channel pressure is real on dense tracks.

## 4. Technical quick-reference

- **New flags:** 0x29F arrival · 0x2A0 stolen · 0x2A1 inspection · 0x2A2 minister · 0x2A3 mikmanc · 0x2A4 red alert · 0x2A5 arrest · 0x2A6/0x2A7 scene-NPC hides · 0x2A8 jail reveal · 0x2A9 jail-door guards hidden · 0x2AA SB3 follower-data init · 0x2AB fairy trial done. Next free: **0x2AC+**. Lantern legacy 0x4E + the follower-data wipe both migrate in `CB2_ContinueSavedGame`.
- **PIPELINE CORRECTION:** `include/constants/region_map_sections.h` AND `src/data/region_map/region_map_entries.h` are both **GENERATED** from `src/data/region_map/region_map_sections.json` (jsonproc) — edit only the JSON + the hand-written `region_map_layout.h` grid. New MAPSECs append after the last custom entry, before `MAPSEC_NONE` (json array order = enum order).
- **More gotchas (2026-07-28):** trainer display names cap at 10 chars (longer = "excess elements in array initializer" in trainers.party); partner multis (`multi_2_vs_1`) REQUIRE `SavePlayerParty` + `ChooseHalfPartyForBattle` first and restore the party afterward — mid-battle party mutations are wiped, so snag delivery routes to the PC in `BATTLE_TYPE_INGAME_PARTNER`; plain warp events do NOT fire on ordinary water/ground behaviors — use coord triggers + script `warp` for surf-route edges.
- **New quest:** `QUEST_STOP_THE_MACHINE` (id 9; storage caps make appends save-safe). Speakers added: `SP_NAME_THE_ONI` (villain red), `SP_NAME_DEFECTOR` (quest green).
- **Prototype additions:** flag 0x2AE deoxys caught · special `WotMakeEventMonShadow` (pair with `setwildbattle`, before the legendary-battle special) · `MAPSEC_PROTOTYPE_ISLAND` (15,7) · Facility-tile recipe: floor 0x202 / wall 0x221 / machinery 0x34E (from LAYOUT_HARBOR) · door metatiles: NightMarket 0x28A is the known-good warp door.
- **Tower additions:** flag 0x2AF oni met (next free **0x2B0+**) · maps `TowerStreet/Mid/Command/Top` (ShinTokyo group, MAPSEC_SHIN_TOKYO) · Badge 8 = `FLAG_BADGE08_GET` + Master Ball granted by the Captain · **orphan trainer pool now EXHAUSTED** (all ten Mossdeep ids repurposed; the Oni's own battle still needs an id — candidates: another dead vanilla id sweep, or the save-breaking MAX bump).
- **Trainer ids:** SUZU/KAEDE/RAN = repurposed `TRAINER_HANNAH`/`_SAMANTHA`/`_MAURA` (Celebi Island keepers). Remaining Mossdeep orphans: BLAKE, SYLVIA, NATE, MACEY, CLIFFORD, NICHOLAS, KATHLEEN, KAYLA.
- **Porymap hazard (2026-07-28):** an interrupted porymap save TRUNCATED `src/data/heal_locations.json` (file ended mid-array; build failed in jsonproc). Restored from git. If a JSON build error names a file you didn't touch, diff it against HEAD first.
- **Opening-flow engine bits (2026-07-28):** special `WotInitFlashScanlineEffect` (overworld.c; arms the flash ring mid-map — pair with `setflashlevel` while faded) · `FNPC_ENABLE_NPC_FOLLOWERS = TRUE` (include/config/follower_npc.h; grew SaveBlock3 — old saves wiped once via 0x2AA) · follower macros used: `setfollowernpc`/`checkfollowernpc`/`facefollowernpc`/`destroyfollowernpc`, follower object id `OBJ_EVENT_ID_NPC_FOLLOWER`.
- **Specials added this arc:** `StartRiftDistortion`/`EndRiftDistortion`, `RefreshPlayerAvatarGraphicsIfOnFoot`, `GetMonShadowState`, `PurifyPartyMon`, `CountShadowPartyMons` (all at the END of `data/specials.inc` — never insert mid-file).
- **Shadow Phase 3 additions:** battle strings `STRINGID_WOTGOTCHASNAGGED` / `_WOTSNAGGEDTOPARTY` / `_WOTSNAGGEDTOPC` (end of enum, before `STRINGID_COUNT`) + table `gWotSnagDeliveryStringIds`; battle-script macros `wotjumpifsnaggedfaint` / `wotcollectsnagged` (end of `asm/macros/battle_script.inc`); natives `BS_WotJumpIfSnaggedFaint` + reworked `BS_WotCollectSnaggedMons` (per-mon loop w/ doneInstr arg) in `battle_script_commands.c`; snag branch in `SpriteCB_Ball_Capture_Step` (`battle_anim_throw.c`, keyed on `BATTLE_TYPE_TRAINER`); Shadow healthbox indicator in `battle_gimmick.c` (`WotBattlerShowsShadowIndicator` → misc pal + `sWotShadowIndicatorGfx`), icon at `graphics/battle_interface/shadow_indicator.png`, purples in `misc_indicator.pal` slots 12–14.
- **Randomizer flags:** 0x4A0 master · 0x4A2 starters · 0x4A3 wild · 0x4A4 trainers. Next free flag: **0x4A5+**. Settings UI = `src/wot_randomizer_menu.c`; core = `src/wot_randomizer.c`. Specials: `WotSetStarterSpecies`, `WotOpenRandomizerMenu`.
- **Trainer ids:** pool at MAX 896 (no new ids without save break). Repurposed: 568, 853, 854, PRESTON, VIRGIL. Partners: `PARTNER_MIKMANC`=3 (`battle_partner.h` + `battle_partners.party`).
- **Hand-made map pipeline:** `data/maps/<N>/{map.json,scripts.inc}` + `data/layouts/<N>/{map.bin,border.bin}` (u16 LE, `metatile | col<<10 | elev<<12`, walkable ground elev 3) + register in `map_groups.json`, `layouts.json`, `event_scripts.s`. Tile recipes: tech interior = General+BattleFactory (floor 515/walls 568,576, border 0x0023); neon city = NightMarket+AshlandTrees (ground 582/void 526/stall 560/mirror-water 283, border 0x020E); warm interior = Building+PokemonCenter (floor 514/wall 523/accent 551, border 0x0201).
- **Gotchas:** charmap has NO ASCII `"` — use “ ”; msgbox = 2 lines (`\n` then `\l`/`\p`); trainers.party held items = `Species @ Item` on the species line; PowerShell vars are case-insensitive (`$h` clobbers `$H`); build via WSL `make` (J: drive slow); `Std_FindItem` clobbers VAR_RESULT — re-check with `checkitem`.
- **Memory (Claude):** `wot-rom-hack-project.md` in the project memory dir points here; THIS doc is the source of truth.
